- Added bmt-wt/.cargo/config.toml (rustc-wrapper plus SCCACHE_IGNORE_SERVER_IO_ERROR=1), so every port worktree picks it up on its next cargo run, including running agents. build-slot.sh exports the same settings for scratchpad clones.

## 10:00 T17 fix round 2 verified at 6f7c56b: ISSUES

- F1 (medium): a blob value in settings makes OpenFailure::of's `_ => Lost` arm quarantine a healthy db and restore an older backup, where Electron opens normally.
  - Decision (Appendix E, fix the root cause):
    - T11's get_setting must read a non-text value as Electron does (a Buffer that compares unequal, so not an error).
    - T17 must classify non-SqliteFailure errors as Unavailable, as defence in depth.
  - Both go in T17 fix round 3 with failing-first tests. The T11 half rides the restack if T11's fix round is closed by then.
- F2 (low): 7 or more backups in one second have the newest deleted by their own prune while create reports success. Fix in round 3: prune must never delete the file just created, and stepping must account for pruned names.
- F3 (low): a folder at matches.db stays transient under CANTOPEN. Decision: keep the recorded departure for chmod and locks, but treat a directory at the db path as Lost, like Electron, because it never heals by itself. Round 3.
- F4: stale golden note and record wording. The uv_errors files are sanctioned. The Windows 0xc0000139 comes from the missing T09 47e267a manifest, which the restack's single build.rs (T18's form) clears.
- Stale-cache risk checked. Compile-time env reads in the port are only CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR and T09's BMT_APP_VERSION (cargo::rustc-env from build.rs). Both sit in the app crate, which dev builds compile incrementally, so sccache never caches it. Its gain is the registry dependencies. Guard and release string checks still compile the app crate fresh.

## 10:05 T08 fix round 3 code-ready at bf79da4 (code 18bb0de)

- States now replay in recorded seq order per launch, from T05's states.ts beside the goldens. Non-finite numbers cross IPC as a `{"$bmtNum":…}` sentinel decoded into JsValue::NonFinite. Shown on real goldens: settings:get 151 and settings:set 23 pass with a stand-in port, and the old order fails 20.
- Decisions:
  - getGlobalChampionDetail(NaN): use a result-side sentinel. Rust writes non-finite f64 as the same `$bmtNum` object, tauriTransport decodes it, and GlobalChampionDetail.champion_id becomes a number that can be NaN. No waiver, because 61/61 needs it. This goes in a small T08 round 4, bundled with T21's contract limits: LiveEvent numeric fields that Electron can send as fractions or NaN, the text/tone order, and typed readStored. T14 echoes the raw value.
  - CardSpec.queue non-finite is refused: accepted and documented. The renderer never sends it.
  - T05 adding data, launch id and position to its indexes: not now. The fail-closed parser works and T05 is mid re-record. Revisit at T40.

- Next: the T08 fix-3 verifier and round 4 are dispatched at the tick.

## 10:15 Re-plan for speed (operator: "the approach of the original plan is making all of this take way too long … speed things up to be able to finish everything")

- Premise attacked: per-unit verify, fix, re-verify loops on 38 branches with drifting bases are the critical path. Every round rebuilds Rust from cold, and integration conflicts get solved again at every hop.
- New approach:
  1. **One integrated tree.** `port/integration` merges every unit tip in plan order (no-ff), using the decided restack resolutions (_design/integration-brief.md). From here, all fixes land on it, tagged `fixup! <unit>` against the unit they belong to.
  2. **Triage.** Fix only:
     - a medium or worse finding;
     - anything that fails a golden, a parity state or CI;
     - any data-safety or guard gap.
       Low hostile-data findings become recorded deviations in parity-report.md, with a batch sweep at the end if time allows.
  3. **No re-verification per fix round.** One adversarial verification runs on the integrated head before STACK-READY, plus CI.
  4. **Review gates batched.** One review package (screens, side-by-sides and a video per gated unit) is presented to the operator at once from the integrated build.
  5. **Linear stack at the end.** The plan's linear per-unit stack is produced once from the integrated tree: each unit's commits are rebased in plan order with `git rebase --autosquash` (GIT_SEQUENCE_EDITOR=:), which folds fixups into their units. Run CI at the stacked head.
- In flight and kept: T05 re-record (goldens needed), T10 fix 3, T25 fix 1, T11 fix 2, T21 verifier, T24 owner. The integration merge picks up their deltas by merging their newer tips.
- Cancelled: the per-unit dispatches queued for the tick (T02 fix, T09 fix, T17 round 3, T18 fix, T20 fix verifier, T08 verifier and round 4). Their items go to one integration fix list after the integration agent's failure table arrives.
- Started the integration builder (opus).

## 20:47 Laptop crash fix d6fbd062 (fixup! T20)

- Root cause is upstream: tauri-runtime-wry 3.0.0-alpha.6's Context is Send+Sync+Clone but held tao's EventLoopWindowTarget, which holds Rc<EventLoopRunner> on Windows, so handle clones on tokio workers raced a non-atomic count. Release builds get silent UB.
- Fix: vendor/tauri-runtime-wry (alpha.6 with main_thread behind an Arc) via [patch.crates-io], with provenance in vendor/README.md. tests/handle_threads.rs aborted in 3 of 4 runs against the registry crate and passes against the vendored copy.
- Decision: accepted. No app-side workaround exists, since every handle carries the Rc. Drop the vendor once upstream fixes it.
- Picked onto integration: 5b6c6681, 0ad9868a, bb066495, 89757266, d6fbd062 (local a2ee4ea3), after an aborted first attempt (the result doc needed bb066495 first).
